devonly, sv
crash
Crash the client. Optional parameter — type of crash:
0: read from NULL
1: write to NULL
2: force an Assert
3: infinite loop
4: stack buffer overrun
5: multiple asserts across multiple threads. Optional number of threads (default 5)
6: looping memory leak until we’re out of memory. Optional allocation size in bytes (default 1048576/1MB)
cmd

npc_
route
Displays the current route of the given NPC as a line on the screen.  Waypoints along the route are drawn as small cyan rectangles.  Line is color coded in the following manner:
Blue	- path to a node
Cyan	- detour around an object (triangulation)
Red	- jump
Maroon - path to final target position
